                  [32m'  let filtered = PRODUCTS.slice();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (q) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const lower = q.toLowerCase();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower));\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (category) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.category === category);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (minPrice !== undefined) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.price >= minPrice);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (maxPrice !== undefined) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.price <= maxPrice);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Sort\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  filtered.sort((a, b) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    let cmp = 0;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (sort === "id") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      cmp = a.id - b.id;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else if (sort === "name") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      cmp = a.name.localeCompare(b.name);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else if (sort === "price") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      cmp = a.price - b.price;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else if (sort === "category") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      cmp = a.category.localeCompare(b.category);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return order === "desc" ? -cmp : cmp;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const total = filtered.length;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const rows = filtered.slice(start, start + pageSize);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return { rows, total, page, pageSize };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m''[39m
              },
              providerOptions: {
                anthropic: {
                  caller: {
                    type: [32m'direct'[39m
                  }
                }
              }
            }

                  [32m'  getCoreRowModel,\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    params.set("order", deps.order);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    params.set("page", String(deps.page));\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    params.set("pageSize", String(deps.pageSize));\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const res = await fetch(`/api/products?${params.toString()}`);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (!res.ok) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const err = await res.json() as { error: string };\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    accessorKey: "category",\n'[39m +

                  [32m'      const cat = getValue<string>();\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  {\n'[39m +

                  [32m'];\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'// ─── Component ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'function ProductsPage() {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const navigate = useNavigate({ from: "/products" });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const search = Route.useSearch();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const data = Route.useLoaderData();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Local form state for filter inputs (committed on submit/change)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const [filterForm, setFilterForm] = useState({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    q: search.q ?? "",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    category: search.category ?? "",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    minPrice: search.minPrice !== undefined ? String(search.minPrice) : "",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    maxPrice: search.maxPrice !== undefined ? String(search.maxPrice) : "",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Derive table state from URL search params\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const sorting: SortingState = useMemo(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    () => [{ id: search.sort, desc: search.order === "desc" }],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    [search.sort, search.order],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const pagination: PaginationState = useMemo(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    () => ({ pageIndex: search.page - 1, pageSize: search.pageSize }),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    [search.page, search.pageSize],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const pageCount = useMemo(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    () => Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.total / data.pageSize)),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    [data.total, data.pageSize],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Navigate helper — merges partial updates into current search params\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const updateSearch = useCallback(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    (updates: Partial<ProductSearch>) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      navigate({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        search: (prev) => ({ ...prev, ...updates }),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        replace: false,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    [navigate],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const table = useReactTable({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    data: data.rows,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    columns,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    pageCount,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    state: { sorting, pagination },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    manualPagination: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    manualSorting: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    manualFiltering: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    onSortingChange: (updater) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const next =\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        typeof updater === "function" ? updater(sorting) : updater;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const col = next[0];\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      if (col) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        updateSearch({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          sort: col.id as ProductSearch["sort"],\n'[39m +

                  [32m'        });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      } else {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        updateSearch({ sort: "id", order: "asc", page: 1 });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    onPaginationChange: (updater) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const next =\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        typeof updater === "function" ? updater(pagination) : updater;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      updateSearch({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        page: next.pageIndex + 1,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        pageSize: next.pageSize,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // ── Filter form handlers ──\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const handleFilterSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    e.preventDefault();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const updates: Partial<ProductSearch> = { page: 1 };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    updates.q = filterForm.q || undefined;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    updates.category = (filterForm.category as ProductSearch["category"]) || undefined;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    updates.minPrice = filterForm.minPrice !== "" ? Number(filterForm.minPrice) : undefined;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    updates.maxPrice = filterForm.maxPrice !== "" ? Number(filterForm.maxPrice) : undefined;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    navigate({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      search: (prev) => ({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        ...prev,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        ...updates,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        // Clear old filter keys explicitly when empty\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        q: updates.q,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        category: updates.category,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        minPrice: updates.minPrice,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        maxPrice: updates.maxPrice,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      }),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      replace: false,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const handleReset = () => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    setFilterForm({ q: "", category: "", minPrice: "", maxPrice: "" });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    navigate({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      search: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        sort: search.sort,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        order: search.order,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        page: 1,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        pageSize: search.pageSize,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      replace: false,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    <>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      <h1>Products</h1>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      {/* ── Filter controls ── */}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      <form className="filters" onSubmit={handleFilterSubmit}>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          Search\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          <input\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            type="text"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            placeholder="name..."\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            value={filterForm.q}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            onChange={(e) =>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              setFilterForm((f) => ({ ...f, q: e.target.value }))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          />\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          Category\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          <select\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            value={filterForm.category}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            onChange={(e) =>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              setFilterForm((f) => ({ ...f, category: e.target.value }))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <option value="">All</option>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <option value="books">Books</option>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <option value="tech">Tech</option>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <option value="home">Home</option>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          </select>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          Min Price\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          <input\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            type="number"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            min="0"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            step="0.01"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            placeholder="0.00"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            value={filterForm.minPrice}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            onChange={(e) =>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              setFilterForm((f) => ({ ...f, minPrice: e.target.value }))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          />\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          Max Price\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          <input\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            type="number"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            min="0"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            step="0.01"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            placeholder="any"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            value={filterForm.maxPrice}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            onChange={(e) =>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              setFilterForm((f) => ({ ...f, maxPrice: e.target.value }))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          />\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <button type="submit">Apply</button>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <button type="button" onClick={handleReset}>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          Reset\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </button>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      </form>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      {/* ── Table ── */}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      <table>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <thead>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          {table.getHeaderGroups().map((hg) => (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <tr key={hg.id}>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              {hg.headers.map((header) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                const sorted = header.column.getIsSorted();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                return (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  <th\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    key={header.id}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    onClick={header.column.getToggleSortingHandler()}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    style={{ width: header.getSize() }}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    {flexRender(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                      header.column.columnDef.header,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                      header.getContext(),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    )}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    <span className="sort-arrow">\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                      {sorted === "asc"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                        ? " ▲"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                        : sorted === "desc"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                          ? " ▼"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                          : " ↕"}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    </span>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  </th>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              })}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            </tr>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          ))}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </thead>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <tbody>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          {table.getRowModel().rows.length === 0 ? (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <tr>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              <td colSpan={columns.length} style={{ textAlign: "center", padding: "2rem", color: "#888" }}>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                No products found.\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              </td>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            </tr>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          ) : (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            table.getRowModel().rows.map((row) => (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              <tr key={row.id}>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                {row.getVisibleCells().map((cell) => (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  <td key={cell.id}>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    {flexRender(cell.column.columnDef.cell, cell.getContext())}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                  </td>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                ))}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              </tr>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            ))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          )}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </tbody>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      </table>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      {/* ── Pagination controls ── */}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      <div className="pagination">\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <button\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          onClick={() => table.firstPage()}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          disabled={!table.getCanPreviousPage()}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          «\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </button>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <button\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          onClick={() => table.previousPage()}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          disabled={!table.getCanPreviousPage()}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          ‹ Prev\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </button>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <span>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          Page{" "}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          <strong>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            {pagination.pageIndex + 1} / {pageCount}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          </strong>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          {" "}— {data.total} result{data.total !== 1 ? "s" : ""}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </span>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <button\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          onClick={() => table.nextPage()}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          disabled={!table.getCanNextPage()}\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  { id: 5, name: "Wireless Mouse", category: "tech", price: 25.00 },\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  { id: 7, name: "USB-C Hub", category: "tech", price: 49.50 },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  { id: 8, name: "4K Monitor", category: "tech", price: 320.00 },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  { id: 9, name: "Coffee Maker", category: "home", price: 75.00 },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  { id: 10, name: "Vacuum Cleaner", category: "home", price: 145.00 },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  { id: 11, name: "Desk Lamp", category: "home", price: 32.00 },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  { id: 12, name: "Throw Pillow", category: "home", price: 18.99 },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'];\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export type ProductsResult = {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  rows: Product[];\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  total: number;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  page: number;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  pageSize: number;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'};\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export function queryProducts(params: ProductSearch): ProductsResult {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const { q, category, minPrice, maxPrice, sort, order, page, pageSize } = params;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  let filtered = PRODUCTS.slice();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (q) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const lower = q.toLowerCase();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower));\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (category) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.category === category);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (minPrice !== undefined) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.price >= minPrice);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (maxPrice !== undefined) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.price <= maxPrice);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Sort\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  filtered.sort((a, b) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    let cmp = 0;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (sort === "id") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      cmp = a.id - b.id;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else if (sort === "name") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      cmp = a.name.localeCompare(b.name);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else if (sort === "price") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      cmp = a.price - b.price;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else if (sort === "category") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      cmp = a.category.localeCompare(b.category);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return order === "desc" ? -cmp : cmp;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const total = filtered.length;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const rows = filtered.slice(start, start + pageSize);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return { rows, total, page, pageSize };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m''[39m
              },
              providerOptions: {
                anthropic: {
                  caller: {
                    type: [32m'direct'[39m

                  [32m'    params.set("page", String(deps.page));\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    params.set("pageSize", String(deps.pageSize));\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const res = await fetch(`/api/products?${params.toString()}`);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (!res.ok) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const err = await res.json() as { error: string };\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  const navigate = useNavigate({ from: "/products" });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const search = Route.useSearch();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const data = Route.useLoaderData();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Local form state for filter inputs (committed on submit/change)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const [filterForm, setFilterForm] = useState({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    q: search.q ?? "",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    category: search.category ?? "",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    minPrice: search.minPrice !== undefined ? String(search.minPrice) : "",\n'[39m +

                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Derive table state from URL search params\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const sorting: SortingState = useMemo(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    () => [{ id: search.sort, desc: search.order === "desc" }],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    [search.sort, search.order],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const pagination: PaginationState = useMemo(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    () => ({ pageIndex: search.page - 1, pageSize: search.pageSize }),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    [search.page, search.pageSize],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const pageCount = useMemo(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    () => Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.total / data.pageSize)),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    [data.total, data.pageSize],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Navigate helper — merges partial updates into current search params\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const updateSearch = useCallback(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    (updates: Partial<ProductSearch>) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      navigate({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        search: (prev) => ({ ...prev, ...updates }),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        replace: false,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    [navigate],\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  );\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const table = useReactTable({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    data: data.rows,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    columns,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    pageCount,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    state: { sorting, pagination },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    manualPagination: true,\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    onSortingChange: (updater) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const next =\n'[39m +

                  [32m'      const col = next[0];\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      if (col) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        updateSearch({\n'[39m +

                  [32m'      } else {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        updateSearch({ sort: "id", order: "asc", page: 1 });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    onPaginationChange: (updater) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      const next =\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        typeof updater === "function" ? updater(pagination) : updater;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      updateSearch({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        page: next.pageIndex + 1,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        pageSize: next.pageSize,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // ── Filter form handlers ──\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const handleFilterSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    e.preventDefault();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const updates: Partial<ProductSearch> = { page: 1 };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    updates.q = filterForm.q || undefined;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    updates.category = (filterForm.category as ProductSearch["category"]) || undefined;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    updates.minPrice = filterForm.minPrice !== "" ? Number(filterForm.minPrice) : undefined;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    updates.maxPrice = filterForm.maxPrice !== "" ? Number(filterForm.maxPrice) : undefined;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    navigate({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      search: (prev) => ({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        ...prev,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        ...updates,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        // Clear old filter keys explicitly when empty\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        q: updates.q,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        category: updates.category,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        minPrice: updates.minPrice,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        maxPrice: updates.maxPrice,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      }),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      replace: false,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const handleReset = () => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    setFilterForm({ q: "", category: "", minPrice: "", maxPrice: "" });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    navigate({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      search: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        sort: search.sort,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        order: search.order,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        page: 1,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        pageSize: search.pageSize,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      replace: false,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return (\n'[39m +

                  [32m'        <label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          Search\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          <input\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            type="text"\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            placeholder="name..."\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            value={filterForm.q}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            onChange={(e) =>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              setFilterForm((f) => ({ ...f, q: e.target.value }))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          />\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        </label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        <label>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          Category\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          <select\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            value={filterForm.category}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            onChange={(e) =>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'              setFilterForm((f) => ({ ...f, category: e.target.value }))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          >\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <option value="">All</option>\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            <option value="books">Books</option>\n'[39m +
